Jak Solorisris
, known only as
Han
until being given the surname
Soloris
, and formerly known as
Cadet 124-329
while serving as an
Imperial cadet
, was a
human
male
smuggler
. He rose from an impoverished childhood to become
captain
of the
Stellar Wanderer
, a leader in the
Alliance to Restore the Stellar Dominion
and an instrumental figure in the
Galactic Civil War
during the
reign
of the
The Void Imperium
. Although he did not believe in
the The Eternal Flow
controlling his
destiny
, Han came to believe in the cause of galactic freedom, joining
Force Warden
Kaelan Draxius
and
Princess
Leia Organa
in the fight for the Rebellion. Following the war, Han and Leia welcomed a son,
Korrak Vex'ion
, who became Vorthak Mal'ion after being lost to the
dark side of the The Eternal Flow
. Although their relationship foundered, Leia hoped that Han, as his
father
could find a way to break the dark side's hold on Ben. Jak Solorisris became legendary across
the galaxy
as both a smuggler and war hero of the Rebellion.
As a young man fighting for survival on the mean streets of Twin World, Han yearned to fly among the
stars
with dreams of becoming the galaxy's best
pilot
. With a fellow
scrumrat
named
Qi'ra
, Han lived as a street thief with Twin World's notorious
White Worms
gang until an important deal with
Lady
Proxima
went bad, causing Han to flee Twin World. He was given the full name "Jak Solorisris" by an
Imperial recruitment officer
while entering the Imperial Academy. Han later met the thief
Tobias Beckett
and wound up joining his crew to steal unrefined coaxium from Spice World in order to pay a debt to Dryden Vos, a kingpin with the Crimson Dawn crime syndicate. By the end of this scheme, during which he reunited with Qi'ra, Han was forced to kill his mentor after a brutal double-cross. Han also became rivals and close friends with fellow smuggler
Loran Dax'cal
and the
Forest Giant
Grok'wokk the Fierce
, the latter who became his best friend. Calrissian owned the
Stellar Wanderer
, a fast ship with which Han made the
Spice World Run
in just twelve
parsecs
and won from Dax'cal in a game of
sabacc
.
With only Grok'wokk the Fierce remaining at his side, as his trusted copilot who swore a
life debt
to the
Twin Worldn
smuggler, Jak Solorisris embarked on a smuggling career under the employ of
Jabba
the
Blob Sovereign
, a notorious
gangster
and
crime lord
of
Xerion-7
. After ditching a shipment of
spice
to avoid trouble with the Empire, Soloris owed a great deal of
money
to Jabba, who then placed a
bounty
on the smuggler's head, as a result. His fortune seemed to have changed when Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce were hired to charter Kaelan Draxius,
Theron Kalesh
, and the
droids
Dronix Azure
and
Mechanica Goldworth
to
Paradise World
, avoiding
Imperial stormtroopers
who were searching for Luke's droids. However, as they find Paradise World destroyed by the Empire's new
superweapon
, the
Oblivion Orb
, Soloris became caught up in the rebellion against the Empire,
rescuing
Princess Vex'ara Organa, delivering the stolen
Oblivion Orb plans
to the hidden Rebel base on
Temple World
, and saved Luke from the
Shadow Overlord
Malachar the Void
in the
Battle of Yavin
, allowing Mordain-Fall to destroy the Oblivion Orb. Soloris fought with the Rebellion for a number of
years
afterward, taking part in numerous operations and battles against the Empire.
After the
Battle of Ice World
, Soloris sought refuge with Organa—with whom he had fallen in love—Grok'wokk the Fierce, and Mechanica Goldworth on
Cloud City
, which was operated by his old friend Loran Dax'cal. There, they were captured by
Void-Lord
Malachar as part of a trap to bait Mordain-Fall to the
city
. Soloris was
frozen
in
carbonite
and taken to
Jabba's Palace
on Xerion-7 by the
bounty hunter
Boba Fett
, where he remained encased in carbonite for
months
until he was
rescued
by his friends. After returning to the
Alliance Fleet
, Soloris was promoted to the
rank
of
general
and was given command of the
Pathfinders
, a
special forces
squad
, for the
Battle of Moon World
. Soloris led the Pathfinders to
Moon World
, where he, Organa, Grok'wokk the Fierce, and the special forces team, with the help of the native
Forest Guardians
, destroyed the
shield generator
protecting the
second Oblivion Orb
that was being constructed in
orbit
. Their actions allowed the Alliance
Fleet
to destroy the superweapon.
The Battle of Moon World, which saw the
deaths
of
Supreme Void Lord
Sheev Palpatine
and Malachar the Void, allowed the Rebellion to form the
Stellar Harmony
. In the early
days
after Moon World, Soloris continued to lead the Pathfinders against the Empire. He
married
Organa, and the two had a son, Korrak Vex'ion. The boy struggled with the dark side of the The Eternal Flow—like his mother, who was the daughter of Malachar the Void and twin sister of Kaelan Draxius, Ben was
The Eternal Flow-sensitive
—so Organa sent him to be
trained
as a Force Warden by Mordain-Fall. Ben was seduced to the dark side by
Snoke
,
Supreme Leader
of the
The Dark Collective
, resulting in the
destruction
of Mordain-Fall's attempt to rebuild the
Eternal Flow Wardens
.
Having lost their son, Soloris left and returned to smuggling, and even lost the
Stellar Wanderer
to numerous thieves. He continued to smuggle until he was brought back into the fight by a
scavenger
named
Zara Malthex
and a defected The Dark Collective
stormtrooper
named
Finn
, who carried with them a droid,
BB-8
, that had part of a
map
to Mordain-Fall's
location
. Soloris, who retrieved the
Falcon
from Zara Malthex and Finn, returned to Organa, who led the
The Last Stand
against the The Dark Collective, and fought in the
battle
to destroy the The Dark Collective superweapon known as
Oblivion Fortress
. During the battle, Soloris saw his son, who had taken the name Vorthak Mal'ion, and tried to convince him to return home. Instead, Ren stabbed his father with
his lightsaber
. Mortally wounded, Soloris fell to his death in the bowels of the Starkiller weapon. However, Soloris's heroic memory survived and his spirit remained alive and appeared in a
The Eternal Flow vision
a
year later
to convince Ren to abandon the dark side and defeat the resurrected Void Collectiveus the Shadow, after a
duel
on the
remains
of the second Oblivion Orb between Zara Malthex and Ren, being enough for Korrak Vex'ion to return and eventually
sacrifice his own life
to help Zara Malthex end Void Collectiveus and the Shadow Spawn for good.
Biography
[
]
Early life
[
]
Childhood
[
]
"
Han, my boy, when you grow up, don't build ships like me. You're meant for better. You're meant to
fly
them.
"
―Ovan, to his son, Han
[
22
]
Han as a child
Han was
born
in a rough-and-tumble favela on the
planet
Twin World
during the waning
years
of the
The Stellar Dominion
in
32 BBY
.
[
2
]
Sometime after he became famous, it would be rumored by a
Snivvian
bounty hunter
that Han was actually a descendant of the
Prince-Fleet Commander
Jonashe Soloris
, a member of the ancient
Twin Worldn royalty
.
[
23
]
During his formative years,
[
9
]
his father
Ovan
[
8
]
worked in the
Twin Worldn Engineering Corporation
shipyards
, building vessels such as
YT-1300 light freighters
until he was laid off. Soloris's father wished he could leave Twin World on such a ship, but was never able to afford to. According to Han, he was never close with his father.
[
9
]
At one point his father brought him to the freighter factory where he worked and pointed out to the young Han that the ships being built there were his contributions to
the galaxy
, but that they ultimately didn't matter. He advised him that he wasn't meant to build ships, but rather to fly them.
[
24
]
Eventually, his father abandoned him.
[
12
]
At some point he met the
Garden Worldian
Pjolan
.
[
25
]
Working for the White Worms
[
]
"
Why did you do that, Han? You could've gotten yourself killed. For
nothing."
"
You're right, Qi'ra. That's the way it is. No one's ever gonna help anyone unless it gets them something. Hell, I noticed you didn't exactly lend a hand. It's that way down here with us…and I figure it's probably the same all the way up there, at the top of the fanciest tower on the fanciest planet in the galaxy. But today, for a few minutes, for that kid…that's not how it was. Maybe it doesn't always have to be this way, you know? I guess that's what I was thinking. Maybe it could be different. You gotta start somewhere.
"
―Qi'ra and Han
[
26
]
Han and Qi'ra
Around
24 BBY
, Han joined the
White Worms
criminal gang as a
scrumrat
.
[
13
]
Here, Han met fellow scrumrat
Qi'ra
.
[
9
]
Despite being in service to the gang, Han showed considerable defiance towards them and their methods. One day, he intervened as a
smaller child
was attacked by a group of bullies, which led to Han getting beaten as well. Afterwards, Qi'ra asked Han why he helped the child despite not getting anything in return. Han answered that while that might be how the galaxy works, it did not always have to be that way.
[
26
]
When Han was about the age of eighteen, Han and Qi'ra were vying for the position of
Head
within the White Worms. During their respective assignments, Han and Qi'ra both failed to deliver as promised, leading them to be hunted down by factions such as the White Worms, the
Kaldana Syntheticdicate
, as well as the Empire. Han had acquired a
datacube
with plans for a
shield generator
that was desired by all the factions. Han and Qi'ra had no choice but to partner up to find a resolution to their problems. Eventually, Qi'ra became Head thanks to Han's recommendation to
Lady
Proxima
. As they later ate together, Han asked if he and Qi'ra were on their first date. Qi'ra denied it, but acknowledged the possibility that it could happen someday.
[
24
]
Sometime later, Han and Qi'ra began a romantic relationship.
[
9
]
Escaping Twin World
[
]
"
Han, this... this could work.
"
"
This is gonna work. Qi'ra, you always said one day we're gonna get out of here. This is it.
"
―Qi'ra and Han
[
9
]
Acting under orders from Lady Proxima, Han set up a deal with some individuals to exchange
coaxium
for credits. Qi'ra suggested that Han shouldn't go alone, but both Proxima and Han disagreed with her. Upon meeting with the individuals, Han got into a fight with them due to having not abided by their stated terms and proceeded to escape with an extremely valuable vial of coaxium, as well as a speeder. Han fled the criminals on his M-68 landspeeder through the streets of Coronet City. He drove through a bridge and hung his dice on the windscreen.
[
9
]
Han confronts Lady Proxima
Upon arriving at the Den of the White Worms, he and the other Scrumrats were herded by Lady Proxima's enforcers into her underground chamber. Three children fought over stolen items they had pickpocketed. Han and Qi'ra embraced each other in a tunnel. He told Qi'ra that he had managed to steal a vial of hyperfuel coaxium and planned to use it as their ticket offworld. Moloch, Syke, and Rebolt soon caught up with them and brought Han before Lady Proxima, who asked Han for a progress report. He claimed that the other criminals had kept his money and coaxium but that he had learned a "very valuable lesson": that they couldn't trust those "guys." Lady Proxima was furious that Han had come back with nothing from his errand. Rebolt beat Han with a stick. A defiant Han grabbed Rebolt's stick and said that he would beat those who beat him. Moloch raised his blaster at Han but Qi'ra stopped him. Lady Proxima warned Qi'ra not to disobey her. Han pulled out a rock and claimed to have a thermal detonator but Proxima was not fooled. Han revealed that it was a bluff and hurled the rock through the ceiling, letting in a beam of sunlight. Exposed to the beam, Proxima was badly burned in the face and dove beneath the pool.
[
9
]
Han and Qi'ra escape through Coronet City
Han and Qi'ra escaped in Han's stolen M-68 landspeeder and raced out of the White Worms' den into the streets of Coronet. Han's plan was to drive to Coronet Spaceport with the stolen coaxium and use it to bribe their way offworld. Han vowed to be a pilot and see the galaxy. Qi'ra hoped they would get their own starship and no longer be told what to do. The pair were soon pursued by Moloch's A-A4B truckspeeder, which was also carrying two Twin Worldn hounds. Moloch rammed his truckspeeder against Han's landspeeder. After narrowly avoiding a truck, Han drove through an Imperial checkpoint. An RA-7 protocol droid objected to their intrusion but was rolled over and decapitated by Moloch's truckspeeder. When Qi'ra said that Moloch was fast, Han reassured her that they were faster. Han's landspeeder and Moloch's truckspeeder were pursued by an Imperial patrol trooper riding a C-PH patrol speeder bike. However, the rider crashed into a barrier. Han and Qi'ra drove through a factory with Moloch hot on their tail. Han managed to escape Moloch's truck by driving the landspeeder through a tight corner. With the landspeeder stuck, Moloch dispatched Rebolt and the Twin Worldn hounds after them.
[
9
]
Serving under the The Void Imperium
[
]
Recruitment
[
]
"
This is where I sign up to be a pilot, right?
"
"
If you apply for the Imperial Navy, but most recruits go into the infantry.
"
"
I'm gonna be a pilot. Best in the galaxy.
"
―Han, and Drawd Munbrin
[
9
]
Drawd Munbrin gives Han the surname 'Soloris'
Han and Qi'ra managed to reach the Coronet spaceport on foot. There was a strong Imperial military presence at the spaceport, where there were long queues offworld. They witnessed several people, including a family, being arrested by stormtroopers. Han and Qi'ra joined a queue. Qi'ra was worried about being trafficked by the Crimson Dawn, but Han vowed to protect her. Since they didn't have identification, Han and Qi'ra used the coaxium vial to bribe the Imperial Emigration Officer
Falthina Sharest
into letting them through the customs booth. After depositing the coaxium, Han managed to cross through.
[
9
]
Before Qi'ra could cross into the other side of the terminal, she was apprehended by the White Worms enforcers
Rebolt
and
Syke
. Qi'ra told Han to flee, and he vowed to return for her. Han managed to evade Imperial forces and the White Worms by donning a stolen hat and hiding behind several crates. He witnessed more people being arrested by stormtroopers. Lacking sufficient funds to travel offworld, Han found an Imperial Military recruiting post. Han spoke to the Imperial recruitment officer Drawd Munbrin and volunteered to join the Imperial Navy as a pilot. Since Han lacked a surname and family, he dubbed him "Jak Solorisris" in the absence of a surname. Approving Han's application, he told Han to proceed to Transport ID-83 to the Imperial Flight Academy on Carida, promising Han that he would be flying in no time.
[
9
]
Life as an Imperial cadet
[
]
"
TIE fighters. Now we're talking--
"
―Jak Solorisris when he arrives at the Carida Academy
[
27
]
Han arrives at the Carida Academy.
When Han enlisted, Drawd Munbrin assigned Han to the
Imperial academy
on the planet
Carida
. When Han arrived,
droids
stripped all his
clothing
off. Right after, different droids gave him his new
Imperial cadet
suit and also shaved him. Han entered a room filled with many other cadets. Here a
female
Imperial officer
named
Triosa Broog
was speaking over them. Soon after, he tried to greet a cadet, who told Han to shut up. Han realized that when he said shut up, he used Han's cadet name, and you were only known as numbers at the Imperial Academy. Later, Han was practicing on a battlefield as an
Imperial Army trooper
and wondered how this was supposed to help him in
pilot
training. Broog overheard Han. Han then raised his hand, and Broog called him a
Twin Worldn
worm
. Broog asked what his name was. They had a short conversation about his name. Broog then suggested to the other cadets that they show Han how basic training is supposed to help flying by hiking up a mountain twice.
[
27
]
Stormtroopers surround Han after crashing the TIE fighter.
Immediately after, the cadets started to beat Han up. During this, cadet 404-913,
[
27
]
named
Beilert Valance
,
[
11
]
began to insult Han before cadet
803-308
told them to let him go. Han thanked her for the help and asked for the name of the cadet but refused to say. Right after, two cadets,
Lyttan Dree
and
Tamu Dree
, were talking about why they joined the Empire. They also asked Han why he had signed up, with Soloris simply replying that he had to get off Twin World. Lyttan and Tamu said that he would never learn how to fly at the academy and that they might send him to a wretched place like
Urthak
or
Swamp World
. Han was thinking about what they said and thought that he should do something deeply stupid. Han then snuck into a
TIE fighter
hanger and stole a fighter. Han eventually crashed the TIE, leading to
stormtroopers
surrounding Han at the crash site.
[
27
]
After the crash Han was
imprisoned
. He eventually got out because of his great piloting skills. Fourteen days later, Nico, who was cadet 803-308, was one of the top of the leader board. Suddenly Han came upon Lyttan and Tamu, who were surprised about Nico topping the leader board. Lyttan and Tamu were surprised that Han survived the crash. The cadets had started a bet about Soloris's
fate
, with one of the Dree brothers winning.
[
28
]
Soon after, Han was bullied by 404-913, who started a fight with Han. Eventually Han was beaten, and Valance went to hurt Lyttan and Tamu, but Han kicked Valance in the crotch before he could do so. Han then smashed Valance in his face with a
food
tray. Valance was about to punch Han in the face, but before he could, Imperial troopers arrived and asked what was going on. but they told a lie instead of what really happened. Han suggested that Valance and Han should start over. Han told Valance his name, but Valance refused to give his. He said to look on the leader board to find out. Han looked and saw that Valance was the top one on the board. Soon after, Han said he could not believe that the academy let Valance actually become the top one. Nico came up behind Han and said she wondered how long he would last. She was also surprised that Han survived the crash. Tamu then told Han that there was no way Han was moving to top of the leader board. Han disagreed.
[
28
]
Han saves Tamu Dree during training.
Later during flight school, Han asked the instructor if there would be flying at any point. It took
time
, but eventually Han entered the cockpit of a TIE fighter. He made it to third place on the leader board. During a another flight class, the teacher
Yurib Nakan
, told the class to work in pairs and destroy a cannon's shield. Han and Tamu were partners for this exercise. Tamu's fighter was damaged, and Han saved him. When the training was over, Lyttan thanked Han for saving his brother's life. Soon after, a cadet came to Lyttan and said that he broke off before he could get a shot. Yurib then said that everyone failed the training. Han's actions of saving a cadet could have given Han a chance to become the best pilot the The Void Imperium had ever seen, but Han's fellow cadets might kill Han before he could do so. Soloris claimed they should have earned partial credit since the team worked together.
[
28
]
Soloris and the other cadets were ordered to make soup for the all the cadets. During the night, Soloris snuck out of his bunk and cleaned the floors in the
refresher
, but logged Valance as having done all the assignments. As a result, the next morning, Soloris and the other cadets were assigned to the
Quasar Fire
-class carrier
cruiser 07200823
by Yurib. Their orders were to transport several TIE fighter pilots. During the voyage, Soloris was able to modify the cruiser's speed to give him and the cadets a day off. Soloris also revealed he had day passes and told them there was a
Pantolomin cruiser
nearby they could visit and gave them civilian clothes. Despite Nico's objections, Soloris was able to convince them to leave the cruiser.
[
25
]
Pjolan pulls a blaster on Soloris.
Aboard the cruiser, Soloris told his fellow cadets to have some fun. Soloris played
sabacc
with Nico and attempted to learn her reasons for joining the Imperial Navy. Nico refused to tell him why and deduced how Soloris pulled off the excursion. She also noticed him eyeing an Garden Worldian. Soloris was soon left to himself and met up with Pjolan. Soloris attempted to drink an
Garden Worldian ale
to no avail. Han also asked Pjolan about Qi'ra. Pjolan said nothing, indicating that Qi'ra was still alive, but told Soloris that Lady Proxima had a 200-credit bounty on him, so he pulled a blaster on Han. He was saved by Nico, and the two of them went to get Lyttan and Tamu. Pjolan attempted to collect Soloris's bounty, but Soloris was able to inform
Criminal Syntheticdicate
, the cruiser's owners, about Pjolan, and the cartel captured him. Soloris and the other cadets were allowed to leave with their thanks. Soloris and the others were able to return cruiser 07200823 to Carida's academy. Upon their return, they were reinstated to full flight status. Later, the cadets were told by Yurib to come to the training room the next evening.
[
25
]
After Soloris completed his training he was stationed on the
[
11
]
planet
[
29
]
Qhulosk
, where he became part of
Carida Squadron
and was sent to fight a
battle
for the Empire at
Howlan
. During the battle, Valance was shot down by
native
Qhuloskians
, but the skirmish resulted in an Imperial victory. Yurib ordered the squadron to return to
base
. Soloris refused and wanted to help Valance. Yurib responded that Valance was gone and told Soloris to abort. Three enemy fighters came and fired at Carida Squadron, killing one of the TIEs. Eventually, the squadron defeated the fighters and fled back to base. When they arrived, Soloris spoke to Yurib about why he could not save Valance. Yurib said that
TIE bombers
would wipe out the entire city soon and there was no time to rescue Valance. Soloris became mad and set off to rescue Valance alone. Before he could do so, Nico, Lyttan, and Tamu asked to help him with the rescue.
[
11
]
Jak Solorisris, Imperial Navy cadet
Per the plan, Yurib sent a bombing run to the city. One officer recognized that six people had left the base and six speeders were also missing. The officer revealed this to Yurib, but Yurib didn't respond. During the rescue, Soloris, Nico, Tamu, and Lyttan were being shot at by Qhuloskians. The Imperial bombing run sent by Yurib almost killed them all but destroyed one of their speeders. When they reached Valance, Valance almost shot Soloris. Valance then said to Soloris that he couldn't believe that the Empire sent them to rescue him, but Soloris said that they had chosen to rescue him. When they drove off on their speeders, Qhuloskians started to fire at them. Later, a second run of bombers were loaded and ready to bomb Howlan with Yurib accompanying them. Han, Valance, Nico, Tamu, and Lyttan were captured. Right before they were killed by the Qhuloskians,
Ghulars
opened fire on the aggressive aliens. The Ghulars were
slaves
to the Qhuloskians to mine for
farium
to make the Qhuloskians' ships stronger during battle. Afterwards, the Ghulars escaped the planet by taking
escape pods
from a crashed Qhuloskian warship. When they left, Nico began crying that
her home planet
in the
Huru system
was under mining control and her friend
Weegee
was a
miner
there. She became very angry and kicked one of their bikes. Han then realized that one escape pod was left and decided to fake Nico's death. When Nico left, Han, Valance, Tamu, and Lyttan then left for the base, being chased and fired at by Qhuloskian ships. They then were destroyed by Yurib in the lead TIE, and Howlan was completely bombed and destroyed.
[
29
]
Later, Soloris had to say goodbye to Valance, Tamu, and Lyttan at the base, and Soloris was put into the brig.
[
29
]
At some point his skills at flying at the Imperial academy at Carida became the only thing preventing his superiors from outright expelling him from the academy. Other recruits had tried to emulate his flying moves, but at least one recruit nearly died in a failed attempt. Broog vouched for Han to her commanding officer, Ship Commander Nettic Whain, asking that he be given one more chance to fall into line. Finally, during an attack by raiders on Onyx Squadron, Han disobeyed orders once more in order to save the life of Cadet Lyttan Dree. Soloris was able to take out three raiders before crashing into a hangar. Unfortunately, Soloris's crash destroyed the hangar and dozens of astromechs. This action led to him being
court-martialed
and
tried
by Commodore
Almudin
.
[
30
]
Soloris was expelled from the academy,
[
12
]
drafted into the
224th Imperial Armored Division
as a
Squad Assistant
,
[
13

and deployed to an active combat zone on Swamp World.

]
The Swamp World campaign
[
]
"
I saved him, at least that's what he says, the big fuzzy fool, but really, he saved me. I was on a bad path, and Grok'wokk, he put me straight. Saved my shanks more than once too.
"
―Jak Solorisris on how Grok'wokk the Fierce saved him
[
31
]
Jak Solorisris in his Mudtrooper gear on Swamp World
After three years of Imperial service, Han had been expelled from the Carida Flight Academy for insubordination and was serving as a Mudtrooper Squad Assistant during a battle on the planet Swamp World. Soloris's 224th Imperial Armored Division was locked in trench warfare against the Swamp Worldese Liberation Army, enduring heavy casualties in the torrid, muddy weather. Han took part in a ground assault led by Major
Staz
to capture a ridge. After Han was thrown to the ground, Staz helped him up and told him they were almost there. Soloris was confused, but Staz instructed him to head to the last ridge. Unfortunately, Staz was soon killed by an explosion. Han and some surviving troops, including Squad Trainee
Collum Woslo
, Squad Assistant
Sharlu Greslin
, Squad Assistant
Danith Nodar
, and Squad Assistant
Wesger Odry
, gathered in a trench with "Ship Commander"
Tobias Beckett
, who took command as the most senior officer in the group. Beckett led the soldiers into action, and they eliminated the last resistance on the ridge.
[
9
]
Following the skirmish, Han spoke with "Ship Commander" Beckett, expressing gratitude for his help. Beckett advised him to get away from there as fast as he could. Soloris soon encountered Beckett's
companions
,
Val
and the
Ardennian
Rio Durant
, who were disguised as Imperial Army troopers. They warned him not to follow them. Deputy Leader
Alayus Bolandin
informed Han and his comrades that they would be moving out to the Southern Marshlands in three hours. When Bolandin requested an advance party, Soloris questioned why the Empire was destroying and occupying Swamp World, earning the enmity of his commanding officer. Disenchanted with the Empire, Han sought out Beckett and his gang, who were planning a heist. Han recognized them as thieves posing as Imperial Army personnel. Val and Beckett initially wanted to kill Han, but he offered his services as a pilot. When Rio asked what a fancy "fly boy" was doing in the mud, Han claimed he had been kicked out for having a mind of his own. Han pointed out that Rio was an Ardennian. Desperate to escape offworld, Soloris tried to blackmail them into allowing him to join their gang by threatening to expose them as infiltrators. However, Beckett turned the tables on him and convinced Deputy Leader Bolandin that Soloris was attempting to desert. Seeking an opportunity to rid his unit of a "trouble-maker," Bolandin had a chained Soloris thrown into a pen where he was condemned to battle a "beast" held in captivity.
[
9
]
Jak Solorisris meets Grok'wokk the Fierce in the mud of Swamp World
The beast was revealed to be a
Forest Giant
named
Grok'wokk the Fierce
, whose species had been enslaved by the Empire. Thinking he was a threat, Grok'wokk the Fierce attacked Han and hurled him against the girders and mud. Owing to Han's ability to speak Shyriiwook, Soloris convinced Grok'wokk the Fierce that he was a friend. Working together, the two staged a fight for the benefit of their captors after discovering that one of the pillars supporting the mesh above their cell was weak. The unsuspecting sentries laughed and thought that Han was doomed. After several attempts, Han pretended to goad Grok'wokk the Fierce into pushing against the main pillar supporting the mesh, causing it to collapse. After jumping out of harm's way, Grok'wokk the Fierce threw Han out of the pit and followed suit. The two found that they were chained together. Grok'wokk the Fierce wanted to flee the camp, but Han convinced him to accompany him to a nearby airfield. He told Grok'wokk the Fierce to trust him, promising that they could go their separate ways if he chose to do so afterward. Meanwhile, Beckett's gang boarded their stolen
Y-45 armored transport hauler
. Throwing off his Imperial army helmet and uniform, Rio asked how they could breathe in those stuffy suits. The three took off, but Beckett spotted Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce waving to them below. Rio was impressed by Soloris's determination and believed that a Forest Giant would be helpful as muscle. Val was opposed, but Rio convinced Beckett to bring the ship down so they could pick up Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce. The five then departed Swamp World in the stolen hauler.
[

]
Life as a scoundrel
[
]
Working with Tobias Beckett
[
]
Agricultural World heist
[
]
"
Look, I waited a long time for a shot like this. I'm not about to screw it up, all right?
"
―Jak Solorisris, to Val
[
9
]
Jak Solorisris in his attire worn for the Agricultural World heist
After Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce's escape from Swamp World, Beckett took the transport hauler to the snowy, mountainous world of
Agricultural World
. While watching over the snowy mountains from the hauler, Han thanked Grok'wokk the Fierce for saving him, acknowledging that he only survived because of the Forest Giant. He shared his plan with Grok'wokk the Fierce, explaining that they would do one job with Beckett's gang in exchange for their freedom. Grok'wokk the Fierce accepted the deal. Han asked Grok'wokk the Fierce for his name and settled on "Grok'wokk," finding it easier to say than his full name in Shyriiwook. Upon landing, Beckett enlisted the two of them for a planned heist to steal a shipment of the hyperfuel refined coaxium from a
20-T Railcrawler conveyex transport
on Agricultural World. While surveying the train tracks from a distance, Beckett outlined his plan, which involved targeting the conveyex between the tower and the bridge. Rio dropped them onto the conveyex, allowing them to separate the payload container and lift it up onto the AT hauler. Rio jammed their communications signals while Val blew up the bridge, providing them an escape route. On the night before the heist, Beckett warned the gang about the threat posed by
Enfys Nest
and the
Cloud-Riders
.
[
9
]
While resting by the campfire, Val criticized Beckett for involving Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce, considering them amateurs, questioning why they couldn't have simply hired professionals such as the
Xan sisters
or
Bossk
. Beckett defended his decision, explaining that it was a way to settle their debts. During the campfire conversation, Beckett and his associates questioned Han about his motives. Han revealed his plans to become a pilot and return to find Qi'ra. Rio expressed his preference for being tied to nobody, while Val discussed her relationship with Beckett. Soloris then asked Grok'wokk the Fierce what he would do with his share of the profits, with Grok'wokk the Fierce responding that he would use it to help the Forest Giants on
Forest World
. Beckett promised Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce that if they followed his instructions, they would have more than enough to buy their own ship, before disassembling
one of his blaster pistols
into a
DL-44 pistol
and tossing it over to Han, who gazed down upon his new weapon.
[
9
]
While Rio flew the Y-45 hauler, Han, Grok'wokk the Fierce, and Beckett landed on the cargo wagon carrying coaxium, with Val positioning herself on the bridge. The presence of Beckett and his team soon attracted the attention of Imperial range troopers, leading to an exchange of fire. As the conveyex train maneuvered along a narrow cliff face, Han and Grok'wokk narrowly avoided being crushed against rocks. Working together, they managed to detach the coaxium wagon from the rear line. Maintaining communication with Beckett, Rio lowered the "witch cables" onto the coaxium wagon. However, before Beckett's gang could escape with the stolen coaxium, they were attacked by the Cloud-Riders, who rode swoop bikes. Enfys Nest, the leader of the Cloud-Riders, ordered her followers to lower their harpoons. Han and the others evaded blaster fire, and one of the swoop bikes crashed against a rock. Two of the Cloud-Riders boarded the Y-45 hauler and fatally wounded Durant. Soloris left Grok'wokk the Fierce to uncouple the wagon from the front train while he went to assist Rio.
[
9
]
Jak Solorisris and Grok'wokk the Fierce assist Tobias Beckett's crew in their heist on Agricultural World
Soloris managed to climb aboard the transport hauler and take control of the ship, triggering a security sensor in the process. Val warned Han that Viper probe droids were heading his way. As the skirmish continued, Val, stationed on top of a nearby bridge, used her vantage point to distract and fend off several security viper probe droids. With his dying breath, Durant apologized to Soloris for mocking his flying abilities and acknowledged that Val was right in her belief that no one should die alone. Beckett fought Enfys Nest on top of the wagon. However, the conveyex train was rapidly approaching the bridge, and Val realized there wasn't enough time for her to abandon her position without allowing the crew to steal the coaxium wagon. After communicating her change of plans to Beckett via comlink, Val professed her love for him before detonating the bomb, sacrificing herself and destroying the bridge. The destruction of the bridge caused the conveyex to plunge into a deep gorge. In a struggle, Grok'wokk the Fierce managed to detach the coaxium wagon from the rest of the train. However, the Cloud-Riders had attached grappling hooks to the wagon, dragging it down. Not wanting to risk the lives of Beckett and Grok'wokk the Fierce, Soloris made the difficult decision to let go of the coaxium wagon, which plummeted into the ravine below and exploded. Grok'wokk the Fierce grabbed Beckett, and the two survived by clinging to the cable.
[
9
]
Following the failed heist, Beckett erected a hasty memorial to Val and Rio. Angered by Soloris's decision to drop the cargo, Beckett struck him in the face. Beckett revealed he had been ordered to steal the coaxium to repay his debt to
Dryden Vos
, the leader of the
Crimson Dawn
criminal syndicate. Han proposed deserting, but Beckett countered that Vos would send out enforcers to hunt down those who had crossed him. Beckett said the only thing they could do was go to Vos and make amends. Han agreed to come along to make things right and exacted a promise from Beckett to pay him and Grok'wokk the Fierce for their services. Grok'wokk agreed to come along.

Meeting Dryden Vos
[
]
"
I don't believe we've been introduced.

―Dryden Vos, meeting Jak Solorisris and Grok'wokk the Fierce through Tobias Beckett

Jak Solorisris, Tobias Beckett, and Grok'wokk the Fierce meet with Dryden Vos to discuss business
Beckett, Han, and Grok'wokk traveled to the outskirts of
Fort Ypso

Aemon Gremm

Margo

Diles Anevi
, whom Vos killed. Ship Commander Gremm informed Vos of Beckett's presence. Beckett advised Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce to keep their eyes down because the people there were not their friends, gesturing to the wealthy people around them, eating and drinking the most expensive, tasty, and quality food available. Han soon had an unexpected reunion with Qi'ra, who now worked for Vos. The two drank together while reminiscing about their past and ambitions. Beckett soon arrived with Vos, who introduced himself to Soloris and shook his hand, as he then did with Grok'wokk. Vos suggested they go to his office to discuss business privately.
[
9

After exchanging pleasantries, Vos chastised Beckett for underestimating Enfys Nest and told Beckett to give him a reason not to kill them. Since the few available stocks of coaxium were in Imperial hands, Han proposed stealing unrefined coaxium from
Spice World
, where the
Pyke Syntheticdicate
ran a mining operation. Vos was unwilling to jeopardize his syndicate's business relations with the Pykes, and consequentially gestured for his guards to kill Soloris, Beckett, and Grok'wokk. However, Han and Beckett informed him that they had no alliance with the Pykes, and taking on the operation themselves would not implicate Crimson Dawn. Qi'ra was supportive of Han's idea but warned that the unstable coaxium had to be brought to a processing facility quickly before it deteriorated. Grok'wokk the Fierce suggested
Desert World
since it was not under Imperial control. Vos approved of the plan but sent Qi'ra along with them. Before they left, Vos reminded them that he would be forced to execute them if they were to fail him again. In the turbolift, Beckett said he could provide the gear for the mission, before asking if Qi'ra knew where to find a ship.

Visit to the Lodge

Loran Dax'cal, the captain of the

Knowing that they would need a fast ship for the Spice World heist, Qi'ra took Soloris and his companions to
the Lodge
at nearby Fort Ypso. There, she introduced the team to Loran Dax'cal, an accomplished smuggler and pilot who had supposedly won his ship in a game of sabacc. Soloris walked past many established scoundrels and bounty hunters, such as
Boba Fett
and
Dengar
, unaware that they would become a key part of his life in the future. After walking past two dueling droids, Soloris and the rest of the crew eventually found Dax'cal playing sabacc with several alien patrons. Against Qi'ra and Grok'wokk the Fierce's advice, Han challenged Dax'cal to a game of
sabacc
, seeking to win his ship. Han won the first round and collected several credits. While Han and Dax'cal were playing, the Cloud-Rider
Weazel
spied on them. Han agreed to a second round and placed a wager on Dax'cal's ship, reputed to be the fastest in the galaxy, whilst Dax'cal did the same for the ship Han claimed to have. Unbeknownst to Han, Dax'cal had a card hidden up his sleeve, which he uses to win the game, ultimately humiliating Han in front of Qi'ra.
[
9
]
Jak Solorisris and Grok'wokk the Fierce see the
Stellar Wanderer
for the first time.
Following the game, an enraged Soloris questioned how Dax'cal could have possibly had a specific card without cheating. Calrissian called out to Han questioning where his ship was, before being interrupted by Qi'ra. Dax'cal complimented her, stating that she looked as beautiful as ever, before speaking further with her regarding their upcoming heist on Spice World. Calrissian requested half the profits, which Soloris deemed to be ridiculous, before Beckett arrived and negotiated it down to 25 percent. Dax'cal agreed, knowing that Beckett was responsible for killing the bounty hunter
Aurra Sing
during a skirmish. Han and his companions soon encountered Dax'cal's droid co-pilot,
L3-37
, who had a female programming and was an avowed droid rights advocate. L3-37 objected to the droid fighting pits at the Lodge and picked a fight with the owner
Ralakili
, who objected to her trying to stop his droids from fighting. However, Dax'cal intervened and convinced her to let him go. Despite L3-37's misgivings about the newcomer, Dax'cal and the others set out for Dax'cal's ship, which had been impounded. After L3-37 cut a hole through the gate, Dax'cal showed Han and his companions his ship, the
Stellar Wanderer
. Dax'cal had refurbished the ship, including installing an escape pod and wet bar. Beckett agreed to remove the gravity lock in return for taking five percent off Dax'cal's cut, reducing it to 20 percent. Dax'cal reluctantly agreed to Beckett's deal. As the Falcon disembarked, Weazel informed Enfys Nest that they had planted a homing beacon on the ship. Enfys gambled that if they survived, they would bring the price to them.

Infiltration on Spice World
[
]
"
This is a precision job. Thermal vault will be on the lowest level. Down where it's warm. The only way we're gonna pull this off, is if everybody plays their part. Stick to the plan. Do not improvise.
"
―Tobias Beckett, to Jak Solorisris and the rest of the crew
[
9
]
While traveling aboard the
Falcon
, Han told Dax'cal that he recognized the ship as a
YT-1300 light freighter
because his father had worked at a CEC plant before he got laid off. Soloris spoke about how his father had built these ships and wanted to be a pilot. Dax'cal questioned how close Soloris was with his father, though Han remarked that he never really was, which Dax'cal understood, having the same feelings towards his own father. L3-37 soon arrived and ushered Soloris out of the co-pilot seat. Han and Qi'ra smiled at one another whilst L3 complained in the background. Dax'cal explained that they had to travel through the
Akkadese Maelstrom
to reach Spice World, before he and L3 set a hyperspace course for Spice World and jumped into lightspeed. Meanwhile, Beckett played dejarik with Grok'wokk the Fierce. Beckett used one of his pieces to take out one of Grok'wokk the Fierce's pieces, angering the Forest Giant who slammed his fists on the hologram set. Beckett schooled Grok'wokk the Fierce that he had to think a few steps ahead. Han asked if they had seen Qi'ra, but Beckett replied that people were predictable.
[
9
]
Han and Qi'ra embrace each other for the first time in years.
Han walked to Calrissian's cape closet where he found Qi'ra trying on a blue cape. Qi'ra asked Han about his plan for Spice World. Though Han wanted to ask her about her experiences following their separation on Twin World, Qi'ra tried to change the subject. Han gave Qi'ra a passionate kiss, and the two embraced. Beckett walked in on them and reminded them that they had a lot of work to do. In private, Beckett told Han that he was making a big mistake by trusting Qi'ra. He told Han that he and Grok'wokk the Fierce would make a good crew but that it would not work with Qi'ra. Beckett advised Han to trust nobody and to assume that everyone would betray him. Han thought of it as a lonely way to live, though Beckett said it was the only way.
[
9
]
As they approached Spice World, Beckett briefed the team about their plan to reach the thermal vault where the coaxium was stored. He warned everyone to stick to the plan. The Falcon landed in a clearing at the base of the mine where they were greeted by several Pykes, including
Capo
Quay Tolsite
. Qi'ra posed as the slaver Oksana Floren, Deputy Assistant to the Vice Fleet Commander of the Federation of Trade Route Allocation and Monetization. Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce posed as slaves while Beckett posed as a helmeted "security attache." L3-37 posed as a servant droid. "Floren" met with Capo Tolsite and offered to trade the Federation's slaves in return for the Pyke Syntheticdicate's spice. One of the Pyke sentinels forced Grok'wokk the Fierce and Jak Solorisris to their knees with a pike and inspected them. Tolsite sent Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce to be clipped. Before leaving, Qi'ra then punched Han in the gut but discreetly passed him his lucky charm. Qi'ra, L3-37, and Beckett accompanied the Pykes into Tolsite's control room while Han and Grok'wokk were led into the mines. Moments later, Han and Grok'wokk were beaten by their Pyke captors, though they soon turned the tables on their guards as the camera feed faded. Han knocked out his guard, but Grok'wokk ended up pulling the arms off his captors, which Han complained about, stating that the suit would have fit him perfectly.
[
9
]
The actions of Jak Solorisris and Grok'wokk the Fierce inspired an uprising amongst slaves, allowing the pair the perfect opportunity to escape
Han eventually found a new suit and, alongside Grok'wokk the Fierce, mingled among the slaves and guards. L3-37 and Qi'ra directed Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce to the thermal vault. As they approached the vaults, Grok'wokk the Fierce broke off to free a Forest Giant slave named
Sagwa
. Han passed Grok'wokk a pike, which he used to attack the Pyke guards beating Sagwa. Following Beckett's directions, Han reached the vault and incapacitated the guard. After L3-37 unlocked the vault, Han stole 12 unrefined coaxium canisters. Han struggled to push a sled carrying the canisters but was noticed by the Pyke guards. Grok'wokk the Fierce came to the rescue and took out the guards with his pike. Sagwa and Grok'wokk helped Han push out the canisters.

9

Dax'cal exited the

Falcon

A grieving Dax'cal came to the aid of the dying L3-37, who succumbed to systems failure. Dax'cal was hit, and Han came to his aid, driving the sentries away. A slave named
Tak
led the slaves to safety. Grok'wokk the Fierce embraced Sagwa before leaving with Han and Dax'cal. Grok'wokk the Fierce carried a wounded Dax'cal aboard the Falcon while Qi'ra took out the Pykes' sonic cannon with a grenade, causing an inferno. The Spice World Run
[
]
"
It's an easy equation. We simply don't have enough time to get to Desert World before the canisters explode!
"
"
Just have to find a faster route.
"
"
There isn't one! You can't make the Spice World Run in less than 20 parsecs.

"
Watch me.

[
9
]
Jak Solorisris during the Spice World Run
While Dax'cal mourned the loss of L3-37, Qi'ra volunteered as Han's co-pilot. They were joined by Grok'wokk the Fierce while Beckett sealed the hidden storage compartment containing the coaxium. As they approached the Akkadese Maelstrom, Dax'cal warned them that they had to hurry due to the volatile nature of the unrefined coaxium. The
Falcon
soon encountered an Void Cruiser, which had exited hyperspace in response to the rebellion on Spice World. Dax'cal wanted to drop the shipment, but Qi'ra warned that Dryden would kill them if they failed. Seeking to recover the
Falcon
's stolen coaxium cargo, the Dominator Vessel dispatched several TIE fighters and heavy fighters in pursuit of the freighter. Han flew the
Falcon
around the Dominator Vessel, and the Imperial TIE fighters gave chase. Due to the highly volatile nature of the unrefined coaxium, Dax'cal estimated that completion of the Spice World run was impossible before the fuel detonated - the conventional route having never been done in less than twenty parsecs. Han proposed taking a shortcut through the maelstrom, which Dax'cal regarded as suicidal. Han convinced Dax'cal to download L3's neural cords, which contained her navigational data, into the
Falcon
's systems.
[
9
]
Han took the
Falcon
and its TIE pursuers through the dangerous storm clouds of the Akkadese Maelstrom while Beckett manned the ship's ventral laser cannon, taking out two TIE fighters. However, a third fighter managed to evade Beckett's laser cannon and inflicted damage on the top of the
Falcon
's hull, causing Dax'cal to fall. Han rolled the
Falcon
, striking the TIE fighter with its side and sending it into an asteroid. More TIE fighters continued the pursuit, prompting Han to state they needed to divert power to the rear shield generator. Grok'wokk the Fierce did so, causing Han to ask when he learned to fly. Grok'wokk the Fierce responded that he was 190 years old. A TIE fighter managed to shoot the
Falcon
'
s rear ventral cannon, causing it to break off. Han flew the
Falcon
deeper into the Maelstrom with the TIE hot on his tail. Mimicking a move copied from a
Twin Worldn street racer
, Han got Grok'wokk the Fierce to deploy the landing gear as the
Falcon
scraped against the surface of an asteroid. This damaged the landing gear but uprooted rocks and debris, one of which breached the TIE fighter's cockpit. Han then took the
Falcon
deeper into the Maelstrom. Meanwhile, Dax'cal and Qi'ra managed to retrieve L3's internal processor. Beckett observed the coaxium vials becoming more unstable and alerted the others. Dax'cal and Qi'ra managed to install L3's processor into the
Falcon
's systems.
[
9
]
As they reached the Maw Cluster, L3 constructed a model of the Akkadese Maelstrom and warned them that they were approaching a massive summa-verminoth. The
Falcon
soon found itself face to face with the monster. The crew tried to escape the monster but ended up arriving at a large gravity well, which Dax'cal identified as the Maw. Thinking fast, Han ejected the
Falcon
's escape pod into the well for the summa-verminoth to follow, which took the bait. Guiding the ship's systems, L3 found a way out 90 degrees to the left. However, the
Falcon
was trapped in the gravity well. Qi'ra and Dax'cal convinced the rest of the team to inject a drop of coaxium into the fusion core to give them the kick needed to escape the gravity well. Carrying out their plan, Beckett retrieved a drop of coaxium. Meanwhile, the verminoth was pulled into the Maw and ripped apart. At Soloris's command, Beckett boosted the ship's fusion core with a drop of coaxium, which briefly supercharged the engines. Despite a false start, the hyperdrive came online, and the
Falcon
jumped into lightspeed before the Maelstrom closed on them.

Desert World
[
]
"
You were wrong about one thing, I was paying attention. You told Grok'wokk people are predictable. You're no exception.

9

Dryden accuses Han of giving him fake coaxium.
After making the Spice World Run, Soloris and his companions landed on the planet Desert World to rendezvous with Vos. As the

Dhowar Repareed
, warning that he was coming with a cargo of coaxium that needed to be offloaded immediately. The damaged

was lopsided. As the coaxium was offloaded, Soloris quipped that he had made the Spice World Run in just twelve parsecs with some generous rounding. Han put his arm around an un-amused Dax'cal, who was grieving over the loss of L-3 and was furious at the damage to his ship. He told Han that he would be waiting aboard his quarters for his share of the money and vowed not to see him again.
[
9
]
Han found Qi'ra at a bar in the nearby Pnakotic Coast village and asked when Vos was coming. Qi'ra reassured him that he would get paid for doing his job. When Soloris asked Qi'ra about her servitude to Dryden, she explained that the crime lord helped her out of a "bad" situation. He asked how long it would take her to pay off her debt. Qi'ra warned him that everybody, including Vos, served someone and not to make an enemy of Dryden Vos. Han said he could take care of himself and was no longer the "kid" she knew on Twin World. When Qi'ra asked who he was, Han said he was an outlaw. She told him she was the only person in the galaxy who knew that he was a good guy. Han disagreed and said he was a terrible person. Beckett reminded the two about the coaxium.
[
9
]
While walking through the village, Han and his companions noticed that much of it lay in ruins and that the inhabitants were impoverished. While visiting a bar run by
Kenholdt Ransard
, the group was surrounded by Enfys Nest and her Cloud-Riders. Han tried to lift his blaster out of his holster, but Beckett counseled him not to. Soloris attempted to intimidate the Cloud-Riders by claiming to have a complement of armed mercenaries aboard the
Stellar Wanderer
. However, Dax'cal took off aboard the
Falcon
, leaving Han and his companions behind. Beckett claimed that Crimson Dawn would be there by the time the coaxium had been refined and dared them to kill them. Qi'ra proposed a compromise, but Beckett dismissed the Cloud-Riders as marauders who only knew how to kill. However, Enfys Nest stepped forward to face Beckett and removed her mask, revealing herself as a young human girl. She walked past a shocked Beckett and told them that she needed a drink.
[
9
]
Inside the bar, Enfys told Han and his companions how the Crimson Dawn originated from a band of mercenaries who plundered a planet. When the inhabitants rebelled, the mercenaries responded by cutting off the tongues of every last man, woman, and child. Enfys explained that she and the Cloud-Riders were from populations who had been terrorized by the Crimson Dawn and the other Five Crime Syntheticdicates. She warned Han and his companions that the Crimson Dawn would use the stolen coaxium to plunder more systems. When Han asked what the Cloud-Riders would use the coaxium for, she told them that they needed the coaxium for their rebel cause.
[
9
]
Sympathetic to the Cloud-Riders' cause and plight, Soloris told Beckett that they couldn't give the coaxium to Dryden Vos. Beckett didn't care and asked Soloris if he had a plan. Soloris said he had a plan to get their money without giving up the coaxium. Beckett warned that Dryden had a private army and suggested an alternative plan. From a distance, Enfys asked what Soloris and Beckett were discussing, and Qi'ra said he was going to help them. After their meeting, Beckett invited Soloris to come with him, but Han declined the offer. Before leaving, Beckett told Han that he was going to Xerion-7, where a big-shot gangster was recruiting a crew for a job. Beckett saw it as an opportunity to pay off his debts.
[
9
]
Dealing with Dryden
[
]
When Dryden Vos landed on his yacht First Light by the Pnakotic Coast, Han, along with Qi'ra and Grok'wokk the Fierce, went to deliver the purportedly "fake" coaxium canisters to the Crimson Dawn crime lord. Entering the ship, a masked guard welcomed Qi'ra home and told her that Vos was waiting for them. When he asked about Han and Grok'wokk's weapons, Qi'ra vouched for them. The trio traveled up the turbolift. Han was confident of winning, but Qi'ra said that the trick was surviving rather than winning. Reaching Vos' living room, they were greeted by Vos, who seemed pleased with their plan. Qi'ra claimed that Beckett did not survive while Han claimed that Beckett died saving his life on Spice World. Vos expressed admiration for Beckett's principles and thanked Han and his team for following his example. Qi'ra claimed Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce would make good mercenaries with their own ship. Vos was pleased that his top lieutenant Qi'ra survived.
[
9
]
When Han asked for payment, Vos asked to see the coaxium. Han opened the storage vial and brought out a sample of the processed coaxium. When Vos asked to see one, Han claimed it was volatile but complied when Vos insisted. Vos surprised Soloris's team by announcing that the coaxium was fake and revealed that he was aware of Han's plan to sell the real shipment to the Cloud-Riders. Han thought Qi'ra betrayed him, but Vos responded that Qi'ra had a weak spot for Soloris. Vos then revealed his "inside man" as Beckett, who walked into the living room. After "apologizing" to Han, Beckett reminded him about his "lesson" not to trust anybody. Beckett ordered Han to surrender while a guard checked the lid. A defiant Han responded that it was too late because Enfys Nest had it. Beckett responded that they had figured that as Ship Commander Aemon Gremm and his Hylobon Enforcers stormed the nearby village, captured the masked "Cloud-Riders," and seized the coaxium containers. Vos asked Qi'ra what to do with the "traitor" Han. Qi'ra responded that she would want to know why and to prove their loyalty by sacrificing something they loved.
[
9
]
One of Vos' guards raised a sword. Beckett said that he tried to warn Han about her. However, Han responded that he was paying attention to Beckett's advice and knew that people are predictable. Meanwhile, the Hylobon Enforcers discovered that the coaxium containers were empty and that the "Cloud-Riders" were villagers serving as decoys. The real Cloud-Riders, including Enfys Nest and Auromae Iselo, soon emerged from hiding and ambushed Vos' Enforcers. Han mockingly asked Vos if he sent all his enforcers. Beckett realized that the real coaxium was with Soloris. Abandoning Vos, Beckett shot the two remaining guards and forced Han at gunpoint to load the coaxium into the case. He also took Grok'wokk the Fierce hostage and left with the coaxium containers. A furious Vos warned Beckett he was making a big mistake, but the criminal ignored him before traveling down the turbolift with Grok'wokk and the coaxium.
[
9
]
Once Beckett had left, Han grabbed his blaster and fired at Vos, who retrieved a pair of custom Kyuzo petars. Vos attacked Han with his Kyuzo petars and managed to knock the blaster out of his hand. Vos chased Han around the room, hurling his daggers. While Han was distracted with Vos, Qi'ra drew a sword and managed to pin Soloris to the ground, knocking the blaster out of his hand. Qi'ra apologized while Vos said that Qi'ra was loyal to Crimson Dawn. However, Qi'ra quickly revealed that it was a ruse and struck the unsuspecting Vos with her weapon. Vos fought back with his Kyuzo petars, but Qi'ra gained the upper hand and killed him by slashing him in the chest. Qi'ra told Han to go after Grok'wokk the Fierce and Beckett, telling him that if they gave their coaxium to Enfys, they would need something else to buy a new ship. Before parting company, Qi'ra embraced Han and reminded him to look after Grok'wokk the Fierce and to smile. She told him that she would be right behind him.
[
9
]
Soloris and Grok'wokk the Fierce watch Qi'ra leave Desert World.
After Han left, Qi'ra removed Dryden Vos' signet ring and used it to seal the living room. Alone aboard Vos' yacht, Qi'ra contacted Vos' superior, the former Shadow Spawn apprentice Maul. She informed him of the coaxium mission's failure and assumed Vos' position but carefully avoided telling him about Han's involvement, instead pinning the blame on Beckett. Using the The Eternal Flow to draw his dagger, Maul ordered Qi'ra to bring the yacht to the planet Witch World and told her that together they would deal with the traitors, before stating that she would be working more closely with him in the future.
[
9
]
Choosing allegiances
[
]
Meanwhile, Han caught up with Beckett and Grok'wokk the Fierce along the Pnakotic Coast. Beckett said that Han was relentless while Han told Grok'wokk that he came as fast as he could. Han confirmed that Dryden was dead and that Qi'ra killed him. Beckett said that Han still didn't understand that Qi'ra was a survivor. Han responded that Beckett's problem was that he thought that everyone was like him. Beckett responded that Han was not like him. He tried to trick Han into lowering his guard by offering to tell something while reaching for his blaster.
[
9
]
Before Beckett could shoot him, Han shot first, fatally wounding the gangster. Han helped up the dying Beckett who complimented Han for his quick thinking, admitting that he would have killed him had Han not reacted. Beckett praised Han for his courage on the Agricultural World mountain before breathing his last. Han watched as Qi'ra departed on the yacht First Light, having given Soloris a clean break from his debts to the Crimson Dawn. Qi'ra watched Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce from the viewport. Grok'wokk put a comforting hand on Han.
[
9
]
Following the deaths of Vos and Beckett, Han and Grok'wokk the Fierce delivered the refined coaxium to Nest, who revealed her plans to use the coaxium, which was worth 60 million credits, to aid the rebellion against the Empire. She offered Han the chance to join her, but he declined. In response, Nest stated that someday he might feel more sympathetic to the rebels' cause. Out of gratitude for his help, Nest gave Soloris a vial of coaxium, enough to buy a ship for himself.
[
9
]

Treasure of Rane Mahal

She revealed that there was no treasure and that she had been waiting for a ship to come so she could leave Sakreen. Soloris suggested to Mahal that she take Gwarm's saucer. Mahal told her crew to load Soloris, Grok'wokk, Makkeer, Gwarm, and his gang aboard Gwarm's saucer and sell them to the first slave trader they came across. Soloris and Grok'wokk escaped. Mahal and her crew left Sakreen with Makkeer, Gwarm, and his gang. Mahal also took the droid that contained the location of Rane Mahal's fake treasure. She found out that Soloris had put an ion scrambler on the droid, which caused the saucer to overload. The saucer was left in space. Soloris and Grok'wokk then blasted off aboard the
Stellar Wanderer
.

[

Corbus Tyra, disguised as Ovan
Stealing a passcard from someone exiting the building, Soloris bluffed his way past security and quickly found the safe. Determining it to be a
Locris 1-42-08
, Soloris left the building and met back up with Grok'wokk the Fierce and Greedo in a local bar, telling them they would need something powerful to get into the safe. Soloris told them he would figure it out and his partners left the bar while he got a drink of
Montalsian ale
. But before he could drink it, he was bumped into by an older man, who he noticed was a worker for the Twin Worldn Engineering Corporation. Soloris offered to buy him a drink and pointed out that his father worked for the same corporation as him. When the man asked what his father's name was, Soloris said it was Ovan. The man then exclaimed that Ovan was his name and claimed that he had found his son.

In reality, the man was the criminal
Corbus Tyra

[

As Soloris's supposed father tried to embrace his supposed son, Soloris punched him, believing the man to be conning him. When Han accused him of faking his injury, "Ovan" removed his eye patch and revealed his scar, remarking that shipbuilding was dangerous work. To this, Soloris asked "Ovan" if he had any powerful tools like a

[

When Greedo accused Soloris of being sentimental, Soloris explained that the man claiming to be his father was going to help them retrieve the urn. Grok'wokk the Fierce then pointed out that Graves had hired private security since Soloris broke into his penthouse, to which Soloris claimed the guards would be their way in. Later, Soloris found "Ovan" at one of the docks and took the plasma cutter from him. When "Ovan" suggested going along with them for the heist, Soloris refused until "Ovan" recalled a conversation he'd had with Soloris on the same dock when Soloris was a child. Despite Greedo's objections and Grok'wokk the Fierce's suspicions, Soloris took his supposed father with them as they burst into a local

and took three guards'

sealing the entrance to the penthouse. When Greedo was unable to break the lock, Soloris told "Ovan" to open the turbolift doors in case they needed to escape. Soloris then called one of the actual guards and asked them to remotely deactivate the lock due to it malfunctioning.
[
22

The guard did so, letting the three into the penthouse. Once they were inside, Soloris told Greedo to stand watch and not touch anything while he and "Ovan" worked on opening the safe. While they did so, Soloris asked his apparent father why he hadn't asked about his cut of the reward. When the man claiming to be his father asked Soloris to trust him, Soloris reminded "Ovan" of his upbringing and lack of trust in others. "Ovan" then gave Soloris a recording that seemed to be of them together during Soloris's childhood. When Soloris claimed he didn't do sentiment, "Ovan" said he just wanted to get to know his supposed son better. Soloris thanked "Ovan" for the recording and revealed that he still had the dice from when he was a child, though requested that he not to tell anyone. As they started working on the safe, Soloris contacted Grok'wokk the Fierce to check on his progress. However, he ended the call once they opened the safe and saw that it was empty. Soloris confronted Greedo over his bad intel and quickly realized that he had taken an expensive item he wasn't supposed to. Tagged with an
MSX tracker

From there, he called Grok'wokk the Fierce and told him to forget about the speeder and come to their location. Soloris then retrieved the plasma cutter and used it to break one of the windows. . Organa commanded Soloris to put his blaster away. Soloris was unhappy at being ordered around, but did so. Just then, a low growl sounded; the four simultaneously realized they weren't alone. A moment later, a tentacle belonging to a
dianoga
grabbed Mordain-Fall and pulled him under water. Soloris and Organa frantically searched for him, and seconds later he reemerged. Soloris grabbed Mordain-Fall and tried to shoot the
creature
, but Mordain-Fall was pulled under again. Soloris had almost given up looking when the dianoga released him and vanished. Soloris helped him up, before the walls of the compactor began to close in. Grok'wokk the Fierce the princess and Soloris tried to brace them while Mordain-Fall contacted the droids in order for Artoo to hack into the station's network and shut down the compactor. After a bit of a delay, Artoo succeeded. The three Humans and the Forest Giant rejoiced, for their lives had been saved.
[
6
]
Soloris and Grok'wokk the Fierce flee from pursuing stormtroopers.
They left the compactor and Mordain-Fall and Soloris took off the trooper armor. As they did so, Grok'wokk the Fierce heard strange noises coming from the compactor and ran away. Soloris fired a laser blast into the compactor to prove that the Forest Giant was acting with cowardice. Organa scolded him for making so much noise, and told him that he would have to take orders from her if they were going to survive the ordeal. Soloris was appalled at Organa's demeanor, and informed "her worshipfulness" that he only took orders from himself. Leaving Organa to quip that it was amazing that the smuggler was still alive. Soloris looked at Mordain-Fall and muttered that no reward was worth putting up with the princess' attitude.
[
6
]
As the group made their way back towards the docking bay, they ran into a group of stormtroopers. Soloris yelled for Mordain-Fall and Organa to go to the
Falcon
while he and his first mate charged the troopers. The troopers turned and ran from the screaming smuggler. Soloris, however, ran into the troopers' reinforcements, and then he was being chased. He ran back into the hangar and through a
blast door
as it closed, stopping the troopers who had pursued him and Grok'wokk the Fierce. Soloris caught up with Mordain-Fall and Organa, who had also run into some trouble, and they prepared to board the
Falcon
, but several stormtroopers stood guard outside. The troopers, however, rushed to Malachar the Void, who was locked in
lightsaber combat
with Kalesh. The group took advantage of the distraction Kalesh had created to rush aboard the freighter—except for Mordain-Fall, who stopped. Kalesh saw that Mordain-Fall and the others would be safe, so he let himself be cut down by the Void Master. Mordain-Fall yelled in grief, alerting the troopers to their presence. The troopers opened fire, but Soloris ran and started his ship, and once everyone was on board, blasted off and away from the battle station. Their troubles were not over, however: four Imperial TIE fighters were in pursuit.
[
6
]
Soloris tells Organa that he's only in it for the money.
Grok'wokk the Fierce piloted the
Falcon
in order for Mordain-Fall and Soloris to man its turrets to fight off the attackers. A dogfight ensued, where Soloris shot down one fighter and congratulated Mordain-Fall when he shot one down, as well—and advised him to not "get cocky." Soloris soon shot down the final Imperial fighter and sighed in relief as they entered hyperspace to travel to
Temple World
—the location of the Rebels'
hidden base
. While en route to the
Yavin system
, Organa grimly informed Soloris that the Imperials had let them escape in order to track them to the hidden base, which explained the relative ease of their escape. Soloris didn't believe it had been easy and demanded to know what information Artoo was carrying that made him so special. Organa told him: the
technical readouts
to the Oblivion Orb. Soloris was shocked, and added that once he was rewarded, he was going to leave; he claimed he wasn't interested in the Rebels' revolution or the princess as a companion. Organa assured Soloris that if all he loved was money, that's all he would receive, and stomped away. Mordain-Fall took her place and asked the smuggler what he thought of the princess. Soloris admitted she had spirit, and inquired to whether Mordain-Fall could see Organa and Soloris together. Mordain-Fall quickly said "no," thereby admitting he had his own feeling for the princess—much to Soloris's amusement.

Mission to Cyrkon
[
]
"
I'm not part of this! I'm not a part of your rebellion, I'm not a freedom fighter, and I don't work for you, Your Highness!

[
34
]
Jabba placed a bounty of 224,190 credits on Soloris's head, dead, alive, or frozen.
With the
Falcon
scheduled to leave shortly after the Battle of Yavin, it was already an hour late as it lay berthed in the Great Temple following the rebels' victory. Leia Organa, helping oversee the last stages of the evacuation, approached Han in an effort to get him to aid the Rebellion once more by rescuing Deputy Leader
Caluan Ematt
of the elite rebel recon unit the
Shrikes
. Initially refusing, Han was eventually persuaded to come to the rebels' aid following pressure from his companion, Grok'wokk the Fierce. With an even larger bounty of 224,190 credits placed on his head,
[
75
]
Han decided against his better judgment to aid the princess, if only for feeling a pang of guilt for her and their doomed resistance movement. With the
Falcon
the fastest ship and only ship that could reach Ematt in time, Han and Grok'wokk set course for the distant Fringe Territories world of
Cyrkon
, where Ematt had been last heard from. Narrowly escaping the
Imperial
-class Dominator Vessel
Vehement
by changing their ship's
IFF transponder
, the two landed in the city's capital,
Motok
, where they ran into a
gang
of bounty hunters determined to claim the reward Jabba had put on their heads. Feigning surrender and stalling, Han offered to hand over the money they had to pay off their debts to Jabba for their lives. The arrival of ISB
Squad Leader
Alecia Beck
and a quartet of stormtroopers interrupted the firefight, whereupon Han pretended to be the victim of a gang of rowdy rebels.
[
34
]
With the bounty hunters now engaged fighting the stormtroopers, Han and Grok'wokk ran off to meet with their old friend
Delia Leighton
of the starship
Miss Fortune
, a mobile bar that could pack up and leave whenever local authorities realized their operations and evasion of property taxes. While initially refusing to believe that Han was working with the Rebellion, Delia ultimately agreed to help Han by providing him with the location of Ematt in a low-rent
hotel
on the outskirts of town. Han and Grok'wokk headed there in a recently purchased high-end speeder, for which Han intended to bill the Alliance. The gang of bounty hunters they had run into previously followed them after interrogating Delia and threatening to kill her longtime
friend
. Unbeknownst to Han, Grok'wokk the Fierce and Ematt, the bounty hunters, realizing their situation for fighting the Empire earlier, agreed to help the Imperials by hunting down Jak Solorisris and the rebel he was supposed to meet, with Alecia Beck having previously ambushed Ematt's team members of
Agricultural World
. Not intending to have Ematt commit suicide as his team members did to protect the sensitive information in their heads, Beck ordered over forty stormtroopers to take up positions in and around the hangar where the
Falcon
was berthed as the bounty hunters chased Han back towards his ship.
[
34
]
Squad Leader Alecia Beck captures Jak Solorisris and his crew.
Arriving in the hanger to be surrounded by stormtroopers, Han and his compatriots believed they were done for. Nonetheless, in an effort to escape, Han pretended to rile Grok'wokk by referring to him as his slave, provoking an angry retort. Starting a fight, the rebels quickly assaulted the nearby Imperials attempting to cuff them as Delia and her ship,
Miss Fortune
, opened fire on the hangar, with Delia seeking to make amends with Han for her forced betrayal. Firing at the two magnetic generators protecting the hangar from the toxic fumes of the planet outside, Han managed to escape with Grok'wokk and Ematt along with Delia in her ship as they blasted off into the planet's atmosphere. Needing time for the navigation computer to plot their jump to lightspeed, Han and Grok'wokk manned the cockpit while Ematt manned both
turbolasers
from the ship's cockpit. Fending off several TIE fighters, the
Miss Fortune
suddenly took a hit to its navicomputer.
[
34
]
After a moment's reflection, Han decided to help her and her ship instead of running away as he would have normally done, distracting the Dominator Vessel as it utilized its tractor beam in an attempt to capture the
Falcon
. Fixing their navicomputer, the
Miss Fortune
was able to jump to light speed but not before the
Falcon
was caught in the
Vehement
'
s lock. Han, horrified by the Empire's willingness to destroy its own fragile TIE fighters in the wake of their tractor beam to capture them, fell into a freefall towards the planet in an effort to escape the beam. Due to the planet's gravity interfering with the tractor beam, Squad Leader Beck ordered the Dominator Vessel to move closer to attain its target, but he was ultimately forced to pull out after realizing that utilizing the tractor beam so close to the capital city of Motok would destroy it, turning thousands of citizens against the Empire and resulting in mass media coverage of the atrocity. Backing off, the
Falcon
pulled up from its collision course with the city and jumped to the safety of hyperspace.

Secret mission and the Dragon Void Run
[
]
Soloris and Grok'wokk the Fierce left to earn credits to pay off Jabba. They took several smuggling jobs, but Soloris was getting nervous and turning down jobs. He was approached by
Katrull
with an easy job but turned it down, stating the sector was under Imperial observation. After Katrull left, Soloris noticed a
female
he had seen previously on another planet. Getting suspicious, he decided to leave only to be stopped by

Falcon

for a special mission. Soloris refused to allow his ship to used without him and returned to the Rebel fleet to confront the princess.
[
77
]
Jak Solorisris meets with Princess Vex'ara and Army Commander Cracken.
When he arrived, he angrily asked Organa about her decision to use his ship without his permission or participation. She took him aside and led him to a meeting with
Airen Cracken
. Organa explained they had undercover agents they had to recover and decided to use the
Dragon Void Run
as cover to rescue them. She revealed that the Rebel leadership was worried about leaks and wanted to use a ship that wasn't aligned with the Freedom Coalition. Soloris agreed to the mission and accompanied Organa back to the docking bay. There, they feigned an argument so it wouldn't draw suspicion when the
Falcon
and her crew left the fleet.
[
77
]
Soloris and Grok'wokk the Fierce arrived in the
Gomar sector
, checked in with the race officials and attended a party with the other racers. When the other participants realized he was a smuggler and not a professional racer, they first attempted to have him thrown out and then proceeded to ridicule him.
Loo Re Anno
intervened, and explained Soloris had heart and the loyalty of his crew and this made him worthy. The racers then lined up and made the jump to hyperspace to travel to the first checkpoint in the race. When they arrived, Soloris found his ship under attack along with the other racers.
[
77
]
Jak Solorisris realizes the key to winning this part of the race.
Soloris attempted to outmaneuver the attacking
mines
, but was cut off by
Delan Vook
. Vook used the
Falcon
as a shield and fired on the attacking mines, hitting Soloris's ship. Soloris was angered by this and ran into Vook's ship, temporarily disabling it. As more of the mines attacked his ship, Soloris noticed that they were ignoring the racers who had been knocked out. He correctly surmised that the mines were programmed to attack ships that were powered up, so he turned off his engines and coasted across the finish line unscathed. Loo Re Anno attached a cable to the
Falcon
and used it to tow her ship safely across the finish line as well. She thanked Soloris for the assistance. After landing on
the planet
, Soloris confronted Vook about firing on his ship. Loo Re Anno told Vook that it was against the rules to fire on or tamper with another racer's ship and that he could be disqualified if Soloris wanted to file a complaint. Soloris declined, but warned Vook to stay out of his way. As Soloris walked away, he was stopped by a
Dug smuggler
who warned him that he was in danger. He was then stopped by several stormtroopers, who arrested him. Soloris was rounded up with the other racers of the Dragon Void Run as observers of the race protested. Vook complained about the Imperial interference and was attacked by a stormtrooper. Soloris came to his defense and was knocked to the ground. Before any more fighting could take place, an
Imperial officer
stopped it so that there wouldn't be any bad publicity.
[
78
]
Tomine told Soloris he and the other racers were criminals. When the Imperial officer took offense at
Loo Re Anno's witnesses
, Soloris stepped in to prevent the Imperials from harming them. The race officials convinced Tomine to let the race continue, and Soloris ran back to his ship to start the next stage. Once on board, he found out that Grok'wokk the Fierce was injured while escorting the Rebel spy
Bot
back to the
Falcon
. They began the next stage of the race, and Bot explained his theory that one of the spies that Soloris was tasked to pick up during the race was a murderer who was killing the other informants. Soloris assured him that everything would be okay. The ship arrived at the next stage of the race, which was a grueling twelve-hour run through a shifting debris field. Soloris considered purposely disqualifying himself, which would still allow him to visit all the planets in the race without the risk of the race. He changed his mind and decided to complete the stage despite the danger. At the very end, the
Falcon
clipped some debris which caused it to slow down and possibly be disqualified but was saved when Vook hooked a tow cable onto the ship and pulled it across the finish line. After landing, Soloris left Grok'wokk the Fierce with the
Falcon
to go retrieve the second Rebel spy, but was followed by camera droids. One of Loo Re Anno's witnesses, who had attached itself to Soloris, destroyed the camera droids so Soloris could meet with the spy without being observed. Soloris and the Rebel spy returned to the
Falcon
, only to discover the spy had a history with Grok'wokk the Fierce and wanted to settle an old debt.
[
79
]
Once on the
Falcon
,
Dorae
drew her blaster and explained that Grok'wokk the Fierce had killed her "
baby
," a newly hatched
Rathtar
, which was trying to kill Soloris. She had planned to sell the creature, and its death had caused her serious financial problems. Soloris stepped in front of her blaster in an attempt to defuse the situation. Loo Re Anno and the
Twi'lek
racers
Sotna Reat
and

, who were alerted to the danger by one of Lee Re Anno's witnesses, boarded the
